# Senda offerings

Pulled from the live books on 21 October 2026. One row is one instrument.
A Jupiter mint is counted once. An xStock or a PreStock that already sits in the verified set is classified there, not added again.
A perpetual, a Hyperliquid spot pair, and an Ostium pair are their own rows, because they are not the Solana token.

**5,105 individual offerings.**

| Family | Count | What it is |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Pre-IPO names | 10 | PreStocks. Jupiter swap. Phantom signs. The token stays in the wallet. |
| Listed shares (xStocks) | 1,374 | xStocks on Solana, inside the Jupiter verified set. Phantom signs. |
| Staked SOL | 163 | Liquid staking tokens, inside the Jupiter verified set. |
| Solana spot | 2,486 | The rest of Jupiter verified. Phantom signs the swap. |
| Perpetuals | 619 | Hyperliquid (every builder dex), Drift, and Jupiter perps. |
| Hyperliquid spot | 337 | Hyperliquid spot pairs. The Ethereum account signs. |
| Ostium (Arbitrum) | 83 | Ostium pairs on Arbitrum. USDC margin. The Ethereum account signs. |
| EVM tokens | 9 | LiFi routes. The Ethereum account signs. |
| Loans | 2 | Borrow against a name or a listed share this wallet holds. |
| Rails | 1 | USDC transfer. Phantom signs. |
| Native chains (print) | 21 | The native chain. A wrapper on Solana is a different row, already in spot. |
| **Total** | **5,105** | |
